## `AGO-6630-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Role:** Backtesting-VaR Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Portfolio Valuation Tracking Error & Liquidity Stress Testing

- **Standards:** `ISO 5725-1:1994`, `ISO 31000:2018`, `Basel Committee on Banking Supervision - Supervisory framework f`, `No. 10`

## `BRB-6630-OPS-R2-OPS-SC-02`

- **Country:** `BRB` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Atomic-Clock-PPS Mechanic
- **Scenario:** In-situ Removal, Reassembly & Dynamic Tolerance Reset SOP for Market Data Dedicated Line & Voice Logger Recording Equipment Wear Components
